Company brief

PIONER BOAT AS is a private limited company operating in Bygging av fritidsbåter. Latest accounts (2025): revenue NOK 88.0m, annual result NOK -7.7m. Annual accounts available for 2011-2025 (15 years). Detailed records only available as PDFs. Revenue stable (-0.0% YoY). Registered employees: 41. Baseline run, no prior version to compare against (run none). Unknowns: hiring (no job sources were checked).
